# Worldwide observing proposal-access inventory

Research edition: 25 October 2026. 363 eligible catalogue entries, including 23 space observatories. Entries include arrays and overlapping programme/network scopes; this is not a count of independent physical telescopes.

Intermediate edition published at the user request while coordinated research continues. Agent usage-limit interruptions and resumption are checkpointed; account availability rechecked25October2026. Successive discovery passes still yield eligible facilities. No worldwide or all-country completeness or discovery convergence is claimed.

Incomplete research edition. Successive discovery passes still produced eligible facilities. No claim of worldwide or all-country completeness.

“Active” means a documented programme continues; it does not mean its latest deadline is still open. Unknown eligibility or fees must be checked before applying. Technical instrument lists are not exhaustive.
